[EVENT: IRN24510]
Date: 2023-10-01
Country: Iran | Location: Tehran - District 11
Type: Strategic developments > Other | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Civilians (Iran) [Civilians]
Actor2: Civilians (Iran) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Coordinating Council of Teachers Syndicates in Iran; Iran Human Rights; Iran International; Radio Farda; Vahid Online; VOA [Other-New media]

DESCRIPTION:
Other: On 1 October 2023, a warning about the observance of hijab by a religious member of 'enjoining good and forbidding wrong' caused a physical clash and a member of the religious order pushed a young schoolgirl in the metro in Tehran - District 11 (Tehran). The young girl fell into a coma. It is not clear how it happened, some speculate she was pushed, but authorities insist this did not happen and that she fainted and hit her head in the metro. After the Mahsa movement, the 'hijab' became a sociopolitical matter, and the religious leaders whom the government and the police support daily try to prevent anti-veil activities by Iranian women.

---

Sources: Radio Zamaneh; Tasnim News Agency [National-International]

[EVENT: IRN24761]
Date: 2023-10-12
Country: Iran | Location: Mashhad
Type: Riots > Mob violence | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Rioters (Iran) [Rioters]
Actor2: Civilians (Iran) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: BBC News; Iran Kargar [New media-International]

DESCRIPTION:
On 12 October 2023, a warning about the observance of hijab by religious members of 'enjoining good and forbidding wrong' and after which a girl was slapped in the face by religious member for not wearing the hijab, caused a physical clash with a group of shopkeepers in the Armitaj Golshan Tower in Mashhad city (Razavi Khorasan). During the quarrel, a religious member of 'enjoining good and forbidding wrong' used an electric shocker. Reportedly, a legal case was filed against one of the shopkeepers. After the Mahsa movement, the 'hijab' became a sociopolitical matter, and the religious leaders whom the government and the police support daily try to prevent anti-veil activities by Iranian women. State violence against and arresting of women without hijab has led to protests in the past.

[EVENT: IRN24847]
Date: 2023-10-15
Country: Iran | Location: Tabriz
Type: Riots > Mob violence | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Rioters (Iran) [Rioters]
Actor2: Civilians (Iran) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Radio Zamaneh [International]

DESCRIPTION:
On 15 October 2023, a warning about the observance of hijab by a religious member of 'enjoining good and forbidding wrong' caused a physical clash in Tabriz city (East Azerbaijan). A woman who did not wear the hijab was injured and later arrested and transferred to a mental hospital. After the Mahsa movement, the 'hijab' became a sociopolitical matter, and the religious leaders whom the government and the police support daily try to prevent anti-veil activities by Iranian women. State violence against and arresting of women without hijab has led to protests in the past.

---

[EVENT: IRN24760]
Date: 2023-10-16
Country: Iran | Location: Dezful
Type: Riots > Mob violence | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Rioters (Iran) [Rioters]
Actor2: Civilians (Iran) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Human Rights Activists in Iran (HRA) [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 16 October 2023, a warning about the observance of hijab by a religious member of 'enjoining good and forbidding wrong' caused a physical clash at the beach park in Dezful city (Khuzestan). A religious member of 'enjoining good and forbidding wrong' was injured, and police arrested 2 citizens due to the physical clash. After the Mahsa movement, the 'hijab' became a sociopolitical matter, and the religious leaders whom the government and the police support daily try to prevent anti-veil activities by Iranian women.

---

[EVENT: IRN25299]
Date: 2023-11-04
Country: Iran | Location: Tehran - District 20
Type: Riots > Mob violence | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Rioters (Iran) [Rioters]
Actor2: Rioters (Iran) [Rioters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Human Rights Activists in Iran (HRA); Iran International; Vahid Online [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 4 November 2023, a warning about the observance of hijab by a religious female member of 'enjoining good and forbidding wrong' caused a physical clash with 3 women at the metro station in Tehran - District 20 (Tehran). Police forces arrested 3 women. After the Mahsa movement, the hijab question has become a sociopolitical matter, and the religious leaders whom the government and the police support daily try to prevent anti-veil activities by Iranian women. State violence against and arresting of women without hijab has led to demonstrations in the past.

---
